Empirically, CAT-OV and CAT-OT outperform existing step-size heuristics in image quality metrics across four text- to-image Flow Matching models, reducing the number of generation steps required to reach comparable quality by up to 40%.

</details>


### [196] [A Study of Conditional Diffusion Models for Open-Loop Control under Dry Friction and Stiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01756)
*Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将扩散模型用作规划与控制中的生成式先验，提出"动作扩散"(Action Diffusion)方法，即以条件1D U-Net生成以初始状态和目标状态为条件的开环控制序列，用于含干摩擦与静摩擦的点质量系统。实验表明该方法在低采样预算下相比均匀随机射击、结构化先验随机射击和交叉熵方法(CEM)能显著降低终端误差与卡死步数，说明条件扩散能通过条件化并重组训练先验中的结构化工件来生成时间上连贯、可克服静摩擦的控制序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型已成为具有表现力的生成式先验，可用于规划与控制。然而在含干摩擦与静摩擦的系统中，运动仅在输入超过静摩擦阈值时才会发生，因此有效的控制序列只占据动作序列空间中一个较小且具有时间结构化特征的子集，传统随机采样方法难以高效覆盖此区域。

Method: 提出Action Diffusion，一种基于动作序列扩散的开环提议分布生成方法。使用紧凑的条件1D U-Net生成以初始状态和目标状态为条件的有界控制序列；并与均匀随机射击、来自同一结构化数据集先验的随机射击以及交叉熵方法(CEM)进行对比实验。

Result: 实验结果显示，Action Diffusion能降低终端误差并减少卡死(stuck)步数，尤其在低采样数量的场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 条件扩散为生成时间上连贯的、能克服静摩擦的控制序列提供了有效机制，其通过条件化并从训练先验中重组结构化的控制基元，实现状态到状态的开环控制。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as expressive generative priors for planning and control. This paper studies Action Diffusion, an action-sequence diffusion formulation used as an open-loop proposal distribution for a point-mass system with dry friction and stiction. In this benchmark, motion starts only when the applied input exceeds a static-friction threshold, so effective controls occupy a small and temporally structured subset of the action-sequence space. A compact conditional 1D U-Net generates bounded control sequences conditioned on initial and target states. We compare it with uniform random shooting, random shooting from the same structured dataset prior, and the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). Results show that Action Diffusion reduces terminal error and stuck steps, especially in low-sample regimes. These results indicate that conditional diffusion provides an effective mechanism for generating temporally coherent control sequences that overcome stiction by conditioning and recombining structured control primitives from the training prior for state-to-state open-loop control.

</details>


### [197] [Toward Explainable and Policy-Aware AI for Carbon Credit Price Prediction: A Research Framework for Emerging Carbon Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01765)
*Summaiya Unnisa Begum,Mohammed Nadeem Ullah,Mohammed Abdul Ghani Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对碳市场价格预测领域集中于欧盟/中国方案、以情感分数压缩政策文本、缺少校准与解释稳定性等十项缺陷，本文提出六层架构 EPA-CarbonNet，采用交叉注意力融合市场序列与政策文本、输出校准区间及政策归因解释。在11年S&P碳指数日度数据上实证结果大体为负：随机游走在5日RMSE上更优（0.0365对0.0475），SHAP排序跨重采样背景的一致性仅rho=0.54，政策注意力与记录在案的监管事件从不重合；但方向准确率58.6%领先所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳市场给排放定价，但该价格难以预测。现有研究集中于EU与中国机制，将监管文本压缩为单一情感分数，且只报告准确率而缺乏校准和解释稳定性，存在系统性方法论缺口。作者将十个反复出现的缺口提炼成影响-可行性矩阵，作为本文的设计动因。

Method: 提出EPA-CarbonNet：六层架构，通过交叉注意力融合市场时序数据与政策文本，同时提供校准预测区间及政策归因解释。研究使用11年S&P碳指数日度数据，并结合随机游走、SHAP重采样背景、政策注意力与文件化监管事件对照等多种评测方式检验。

Result: 结果以“如实报告”为原则且大体为负：随机游走在5日RMSE上胜过模型（0.0365对0.0475）；SHAP重要性排序在不同重采样背景间仅中度一致（rho=0.54）；政策注意力从未与记录在案的监管事件重合。仅方向准确率达58.6%，领先全部基线。代码、数据文档与全部结果工件已开源。

Conclusion: 负结果提示：在碳价预测中融合政策文本的复杂模型未必优于随机游走，且解释稳定性与政策注意力可信度存疑；但方向准确率的优势说明模型在涨跌方向判断上仍有价值。作者通过完整公开工件，倡导以校准、解释稳定性与负结果透明报告为标准的研究范式。

Abstract: Carbon markets put a price on emissions, yet that price remains hard to forecast. Work in this area clusters on the EU and Chinese schemes, compresses regulatory text into a sentiment score, and reports accuracy without calibration or explanation stability. We distil ten recurring gaps into an impact-feasibility matrix and propose EPA-CarbonNet, a six-layer architecture that fuses market series with policy text by cross-attention and calibrated intervals alongside policy-attributed explanations. We then build and test it on eleven years of daily S and P carbon index data. The findings are largely negative, and reported as measured: a random walk beats the model on five-day RMSE (0.0365 against 0.0475), SHAP rankings agree at rho = 0.54 across resampled backgrounds, and policy attention never coincides with documented regulatory events. Directional accuracy, at 58.6 percent, leads every baseline. Code, data documentation and all result artifacts are available at https://github.com/Kimalice/Toward-Explainable-and-Policy-Aware-AI-for-Carbon-Credit-Price-Prediction

</details>


### [198] [Emergence of Fibrations, Compression, and Symmetry Breaking in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01768)
*Osvaldo M Velarde,Lucas C Parra,Alireza Hashemi,Hernan A Makse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明深度神经网络中的学习会产生图论意义上的覆盖对称性（covering symmetries），该对称性是随机梯度下降的稳定吸引子；它广泛存在于多层、卷积、循环和 Transformer 等主流架构中，既可用于将网络压缩至原规模 17% 而不损失性能，又可通过受控破缺克服学习可塑性丧失，在持续学习中取得最优性能，从而将黑箱网络转化为可解释的有色图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络常被视为强大但不透明的黑箱，缺乏可解释性与高效推理的理论基础；同时持续学习中的可塑性丧失问题亟待解决。作者希望从图论对称性出发，为深度学习提供新的理论支撑。

Method: 作者利用图论中的纤维化（fibration）与覆盖（covering）概念刻画深度网络中的局部对称性；通过理论证明覆盖对称性是随机梯度下降的稳定吸引子，并在多层、卷积、循环和 Transformer 网络中进行实证验证；进一步利用这些对称性进行模型压缩，并通过受控的对称性破缺来应对持续学习中的可塑性丧失。

Result: 理论与实证一致地展示了覆盖对称性在多种主流架构中的涌现；利用对称性可将网络压缩至原有规模的 17% 且不损失性能；受控破缺覆盖对称性克服了学习可塑性丧失，在持续学习任务上达到目前最优性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于对称性的人工智能系统提供了新基础，能够将黑箱网络转化为可解释的有色图，从而实现更高效的推理与终身学习。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks are often regarded as powerful yet opaque black boxes. Here, we demonstrate that learning in deep neural networks generates local symmetries known in graph theory as fibrations and coverings. We prove that covering symmetries are stable attractors of stochastic gradient descent. Consistent with this theory, we report the emergence of covering symmetries across major network architectures, including multilayer, convolutional, recurrent, and transformer networks. Exploiting these symmetries enables drastic model compression - reducing networks to 17% of their original size without sacrificing performance. Furthermore, controlled breaking of covering symmetry overcomes the loss of plasticity, achieving state-of-the-art performance in continual learning. The theoretical results provide a new foundation for AI systems based on symmetries that convert black boxes into interpretable colored graphs and enable more efficient inference and lifelong learning.

</details>


### [199] [D-FROST: Decentralized Federated pRompt-tuning via Optimal tranSporT for Non-IID and Imbalanced Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01802)
*Quan Minh Nguyen,Hoang M. Ngo,Trong Nghia Hoang,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中的提示词微调（prompt tuning），提出基于最优传输（OT）的去中心化提示词微调算法D-FROST。作者将去中心化提示词微调建模为基于Wasserstein距离的提示词测度优化问题，通过传输匹配合并邻域提示词为紧凑代表集，并给出了Wasserstein共识误差界以及网络级提示词重心收敛到平稳邻域的保证。实验验证了其在异构客户端数据下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示词微调只需冻结预训练骨干并更新少量可学习提示词，非常适合通信开销昂贵的去中心化联邦学习（DFL）。然而，从异构局部数据中学到的提示词集合在索引上可能不对齐，导致标准的去中心化平均操作不适用；同时算法需要具备达成共识并朝共享目标推进的理论保证。目前尚缺乏对DFL中提示词微调的系统性研究。

Method: 将去中心化提示词微调形式化为提示词测度上的Wasserstein优化问题，以刻画提示词的集合值结构；据此提出D-FROST算法，利用基于传输的匹配（transportation-based matching）将邻域提示词合并为紧凑的代表性提示词集合；并给出Wasserstein共识误差界及网络级提示词重心收敛到平稳邻域的理论分析。

Result: 在异构客户端数据实验中，D-FROST在去中心化提示词微调任务上展现了有效性；理论上证明了客户端间的Wasserstein共识误差有界，且网络级提示词重心收敛到驻点的邻域。

Conclusion: D-FROST为DFL中的提示词微调提供了首个理论基础与有效算法，通过最优传输解决了提示词索引不对齐和共识达成的问题，兼顾了参数效率、通信效率与收敛性保证。

Abstract: Prompt tuning provides a parameter-efficient way to adapt foundation models (FMs) by freezing the pretrained backbone and updating only a small set of learnable prompts. This property makes prompt tuning especially suitable for decentralized federated learning (DFL), where exchanging full-model updates can be prohibitively expensive. However, prompt tuning in DFL introduces new challenges. Prompt sets learned from heterogeneous local data may not be index-wise aligned, making standard decentralized averaging unsuitable. In addition, the algorithm should be theoretically guaranteed to achieve consensus and make progress toward the shared objective. In this work, we provide the first study of prompt tuning in DFL. We formulate decentralized prompt tuning as a Wasserstein-based optimization problem over prompt measures, which captures the set-valued structure of prompts. We then propose D-FROST, an optimal-transport-based (OT-based) decentralized prompt-tuning algorithm that merges neighborhood prompts into compact representative prompt sets through transportation-based matching. We further analyze D-FROST by bounding the Wasserstein consensus error across clients, and establishing convergence of the network-level prompt barycenter to a neighborhood of stationarity. Experiments under heterogeneous client data demonstrate the effectiveness of D-FROST for decentralized prompt tuning.

</details>


### [200] [hLLM: Single Pass Decoding for Generative Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01807)
*Emil Laftchiev,Prachi Agrawal,Moe Kayali,Bixing Yan,Qi Xu,Zijie Lei,Chen Qiu,Zhi Hua,Ke Li,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 hLLM（Hungarian LLM），一种针对排序任务的格式特化解码策略：通过轻量级自注意力头从 LLM 的 prefill 隐藏状态中读取 N×K 的"物品-位置"得分矩阵，再利用匈牙利算法将其解码为最优二部图匹配，从而直接得到有效排列，将传统自回归逐 token 解码（需要进行 N 次序列前向传播）压缩为 O(1) 次前向传播，最终实现 64 倍推理加速且排序质量与教师模型持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在生成式排序任务中已达到最先进水平，但其解码方式——自回归逐 token 生成——每生成一个 token 就需一次序列前向传播，计算开销极高。作者观察到：排序器实际只需输出 N 个序数值（即按排序顺序命名的物品），这种排列结构的窄输出格式并不适合从左到右的生成式解码，存在大幅提效的空间。

Method: 核心方法分三步：(1) 利用轻量级自注意力头从 LLM 的 prefill 隐藏状态直接读取一个 N×K 的物品-位置得分矩阵；(2) 将该矩阵视为二分图匹配的权重，用匈牙利算法求最优分配，从而直接解码出 N 个序数值；(3) 结合 LoRA 微调与教师排序蒸馏（teacher ranking distillation）对骨干模型进行适配。由于匈牙利算法输出天然是有效排列，无需事后修复（repair）。作者还对该框架的架构、训练信号和骨干适配进行了系统性消融研究。

Result: 经系统性研究训练信号与骨干适配方式后，LoRA 微调结合教师排序蒸馏可将端到端推理时间降至 28 ms，较传统解码提速 64 倍，同时保持与教师相当的排序质量；完整消融实验分别量化了架构、训练信号与骨干适配三项贡献。

Conclusion: hLLM 将生成式排序与组合优化联系起来，证明了排列结构的排序输出可以绕过自回归解码，以 O(1) 次前向传播完成解码，为实时排序场景下的其他格式特化解码机制开辟了新路径。该工作表明：针对任务输出结构的解码策略设计是提升 LLM 推理效率的重要方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art generative ranking quality, but the ranking they produce must be decoded, and autoregressive decoding spends one sequential forward pass per emitted token. We observe that the only tokens a ranker must emit are the $N$ ordinal values naming the items in ranked order, and that this narrow, permutation-structured output format admits decoding strategies which are much more efficient than left-to-right generation. We introduce hLLM (Hungarian LLM), a format-specialized decoding strategy that decodes all $N$ ordinals in $O(1)$ forward passes. hLLM reads an $N \times K$ item-position score matrix off the LLM's prefill hidden states with a lightweight self-attention head, then decodes the ordinals as the optimal bipartite assignment of that matrix via the Hungarian algorithm, yielding a valid permutation by construction rather than by repair. Through a systematic study of training signals and backbone adaptation, we show that LoRA-based fine-tuning combined with teacher ranking distillation reaches 28 ms end-to-end inference, a speed-up of $64\times$ while maintaining ranking quality on par with the teacher. We provide a complete ablation decomposing the contributions of architecture, training signal, and backbone adaptation. Our framework connects generative ranking to combinatorial optimization, opening a path toward other $O(1)$-decode mechanisms for real-time ranking.

</details>


### [201] [Import What You Need: Learning When and How to Augment EHR Graphs with External Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01839)
*Chen Chen,Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Dongjie Wang,Mei Liu,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ReTA，一种基于强化学习的动态拓扑增强框架，将知识图谱导入建模为每次就诊的、受预算约束的策略，通过软导入、硬导入和跳过三种动作自适应地决定是否以及如何将知识图谱知识融入 EHR 预测，在 MIMIC-III/IV 上显著优于基线且更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR 纵向预测受患者轨迹稀疏和不规则的限制，外部知识图谱增强可缓解该问题；但现有方法大多采用固定、上下文无关的拓扑增强，即对任何患者和任何时刻都添加相同的 KG 节点与边，不考虑患者状态的动态演化，导致知识导入缺乏针对性和成本控制。

Method: ReTA 将知识图谱导入建模为每次就诊的、受预算约束的策略：1) 离线构建经精炼的 KG 模板池；2) 学习策略从三种增强动作中为每次就诊选择一个——Soft Import（仅丰富节点特征、不改拓扑）、Hard Import（将紧凑 KG 子图嫁接至就诊图以创建消息传递捷径）、Skip（当基础编码器已足够自信时不增强）；3) 采用解耦编码器，在独立通道中处理语义与结构信号，并通过自适应门控融合，以稳定学习。

Result: 在 MIMIC-III 和 MIMIC-IV 上的诊断预测、死亡率和再入院任务中，ReTA 一致优于强基线，同时保持高效；可跨数据集和知识图谱迁移，并产生可解释的增强模式；在稀疏监督下的稳健增益凸显了动态知识导入决策在提升准确性的同时控制成本的优势。

Conclusion: 面向 EHR 纵向预测，动态、上下文感知且受预算约束的知识图谱增强优于固定拓扑增强：ReTA 通过在每次就诊自适应决定知识导入方式（软导入/硬导入/跳过），在提升预测精度、节省计算成本、增强可解释性与跨域迁移能力方面均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Longitudinal prediction from electronic health records (EHRs) is limited by the sparsity and irregularity in patient trajectories, and knowledge augmentation with external knowledge graphs (KGs) offers a promising way to alleviate these issues. However, most existing methods perform fixed, context-agnostic topology augmentation by adding the same KG nodes and edges regardless of a patient's evolving state. We propose ReTA, a Reinforcement learning-based dynamic Topology Augmentation framework that casts KG import as a per-visit, budget-aware policy. ReTA first constructs an offline refined pool of KG-grounded templates, then learns a policy to select one augment action per visit from three options: Soft Import, which enriches node features without modifying graph topology, Hard Import, which grafts a compact KG subgraph onto the visit graph to create message-passing shortcuts, and Skip, which leaves the visit unaugmented when the base encoder is already confident. To stabilize learning, ReTA employs a decoupled encoder that processes semantic and structural signals in separate channels and fuses them via adaptive gating. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV across diagnosis prediction, mortality, and readmission show that ReTA consistently outperforms strong baselines while remaining efficient, transfers across datasets and knowledge graphs, and yields interpretable augmentation patterns. The robust gains under sparse supervision highlight the advantage of ReTA's dynamic decision to import knowledge, boosting accuracy while curbing costs.

</details>


### [202] [OutageDiT: A Generative Foundation Model for Power Outage Forecasting and Scenario Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01896)
*Yunqin Zhu,Feng Qiu,Yao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OutageDiT，一个基于全美停电与天气数据训练的深度生成式基础模型，能够以 15 分钟分辨率生成七天停电轨迹，统一支持点预测、不确定性量化与条件事件模拟，并在保留时序依赖的同时实现零样本迁移到未参与训练的地区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停电规划需要在事件发生之前生成反映严重程度、时间与持续时间不确定性的场景，同时保持时序依赖。然而极端事件十分罕见，单一地区的数据中严重停电与恢复模式的样本极少，难以支撑可靠的情景生成。

Method: 条件编码器对历史上下文和已知未来协变量只编码一次，浅层流解码器复用对齐到预测时域的状态来一次性生成完整轨迹，从而在单一深度生成模型内同时支持点预测、不确定性量化与条件事件模拟。

Result: 在停电预测基准上，OutageDiT 相较于强基线显著提升了预测精度与场景质量，并支持对保留地区的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 条件停电模拟可作为连接停电预测与不确定性下运行规划的桥梁，为极端事件发生前的停电规划提供可行的场景生成方案。

Abstract: Power-outage planning requires scenarios before an event occurs. These scenarios must represent uncertainty in magnitude, timing, and duration while preserving temporal dependence. However, severe events are rare, and data from any single region contain few examples of extreme outage and restoration patterns. To address this challenge, we introduce OutageDiT, a foundation model for generating seven-day outage trajectories at quarter-hour resolution, trained on outage and weather records across the United States. Specifically, a condition encoder processes the historical context and known future covariates once per forecast, and a shallow flow decoder reuses the resulting horizon-aligned states to generate complete trajectories. The resulting samples support point forecasting, uncertainty quantification, and conditional event simulation within one deep generative model. Across outage forecasting benchmarks, OutageDiT improves forecast accuracy and scenario quality over strong baselines and supports zero-shot transfer to held-out regions. Together, these results position conditional outage simulation as a bridge from outage forecasting to operational planning under uncertainty.

</details>


### [203] [CRISP: Cliff-awaRe Input-adaptive Sparse Prefilling with Structural-Mass-Motivated Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01925)
*Huu Huy Nguyen,Chien Van Nguyen,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Linh Ngo Van,Jieyang Chen,Thien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRISP 提出一种面向长上下文 LLM 注意力预填充阶段的输入自适应稀疏注意力方法,通过结构代理 C_struct 替代 JSD 路由以消除计算开销,并基于噪声底限设计 sink-aware 阈值,理论上证明并消除严格累积覆盖阈值在长上下文中引入的 O(n) 背景噪声,从而在 InfiniteBench、RULER 和 LongBench 上成为最强稀疏方法,并在检索密集任务上达到或超越精确稠密注意力,最高获得 5.30 倍注意力加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 注意力预填充阶段的计算复杂度随序列长度二次增长,使自注意力成为严重瓶颈。传统稀疏注意力依赖固定模式或离线分析,无法适应输入相关的注意力结构;而近期动态方法虽能实时将头路由到稀疏模式,却依赖间接路由代理带来额外开销,且其预算分配机制忽视了 softmax 后的质量(概率质量)层级结构。作者指出动态路由范式存在两个结构性问题:路由决策可从代理注意力图结构中直接读取,以及严格累积覆盖阈值会在长上下文中累积 O(n) 背景噪声。

Method: CRISP 从两方面解决动态路由的结构性挑战。其一,用结构代理 C_struct 替代 Jensen-Shannon Divergence (JSD) 路由:该代理度量 Vertical-Slash 兼容位置上的质量,能复现 JSD 的路由决策,同时消除 pooled matmul 与后续 KL 散度计算开销。其二,作者形式化 post-softmax 质量悬崖,并在理论上证明严格累积覆盖阈值在长上下文中会累积 O(n) 背景噪声;CRISP 通过基于噪声底限的 sink-aware 阈值来规避该问题。

Result: 在 InfiniteBench、RULER 和 LongBench 上对两个模型家族进行实验,CRISP 是整体上最强的稀疏方法,在检索密集基准上达到或超过精确稠密注意力;相对于基线在检索任务上最多恢复 +28.0 个百分点,并在 512k token 上下文下实现最高 5.30 倍注意力加速。加速主要来自选择阶段 O(n) 噪声消除,同时保持结构完整性。

Conclusion: 作者认为,动态稀疏路由的性能不仅取决于选择哪些位置,还取决于如何分配预算与度量代理;直接从代理注意力图结构读取路由决策并依据噪声底限设定阈值,可以既消除路由开销又排除背景噪声干扰。CRISP 验证了这一思路:在不牺牲结构完整性的前提下,以更低开销实现比肩甚至超越稠密注意力的检索性能与显著加速,为长上下文预填充阶段的稀疏化提供了更高效、输入自适应的方案。

Abstract: The attention prefilling phase of long-context LLM inference scales quadratically, making self-attention a severe computational bottleneck. Traditional sparse attention methods mitigate this through fixed patterns or offline profiling, but lack the flexibility to adapt to input-dependent attention structure. Recent dynamic methods address this by routing heads to sparse patterns in real-time, but rely on indirect routing proxies with overhead and budget allocation mechanisms that overlook the post-softmax mass hierarchy. We present CRISP (Cliff-awaRe Input-adaptive Sparse Prefilling), which identifies and addresses two structural challenges in this dynamic routing paradigm. First, we show that the routing decision can be read directly off the structure of the proxy attention map. We replace the Jensen-Shannon Divergence (JSD) routing with C_struct, a structural proxy that measures mass at Vertical-Slash compatible positions and reproduces JSD's routing decisions while eliminating both the pooled matmul and subsequent KL divergence overhead. Second, we formalize the post-softmax mass cliff and demonstrate theoretically that strictly cumulative coverage thresholds accumulate O(n) background noise at long contexts. CRISP navigates this via a sink-aware threshold grounded in the noise floor. Empirically, across InfiniteBench, RULER and LongBench on two model families, CRISP is the strongest sparse method overall and matches or exceeds exact dense attention on retrieval-heavy benchmarks, recovering up to +28.0 pp on retrieval tasks over baselines and achieving up to a 5.30x attention speedup at 512k tokens, driven primarily by our O(n) noise elimination during selection while preserving structural integrity.

</details>


### [204] [OR-Transformer: Scaling Real-Time Decision-Making to 1,000 Items](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01933)
*Shuze Daniel Liu,David Simchi-Levi,Claire Chen,Chutong Gao,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OR-Transformer，一种面向大规模随机需求联合补货问题的深度强化学习框架，采用物品排列等变的 Transformer 架构，并通过库存动态的路径梯度（pathwise gradient）进行训练。在多达 1,024 个库存物品的规模上，OR-Transformer 随规模增大越来越优于基于学习的基线和滚动时域 MILP 基线，并将在线决策时间相比 MILP 求解器缩短超过 400 万倍，从而支持供应链运营中的实时大规模深度强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链补货需要协调数千个异构物品的补货决策，涉及相关随机需求、异构提前期和共享固定订购成本，观测空间超过 10^4 维。在此规模下，滚动时域随机混合整数线性规划（MILP）运算过慢，而标准强化学习（RL）方法在高维动作空间中面临日益严峻的信用分配问题，亟需可扩展且高效的求解框架。

Method: 作者设计了 OR-Transformer：一种深度强化学习框架，其核心为物品排列等变（item-permutation-equivariant）的 Transformer 架构，并采用通过库存动态进行路径梯度（pathwise gradient）的训练方式，以端到端地学习联合补货策略。

Result: 在多达 1,024 个库存物品的问题规模上，OR-Transformer 随规模增大持续超越基于学习的基线与滚动时域 MILP 基线；相比 MILP 求解器，其在线决策时间降低超过 400 万倍，可实现在线实时决策。

Conclusion: OR-Transformer 展示了在超大规模联合补货问题中深度 RL 的有效性与实用性：等变架构与路径梯度训练相结合，使方法在规模扩展时性能持续领先，并凭借极低的在线推理成本支持供应链运营中的实时大规模部署。

Abstract: Modern supply chain operations can require coordinating replenishment across thousands of heterogeneous items under correlated stochastic demand, heterogeneous lead times, and shared fixed ordering costs, yielding observation spaces exceeding $10^4$ dimensions. At this scale, rolling-horizon stochastic mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs) become prohibitively slow, while standard reinforcement learning (RL) methods face increasingly challenging credit assignment in high-dimensional action spaces. We introduce OR-Transformer, a deep reinforcement learning framework for joint replenishment under stochastic demand, with an item-permutation-equivariant Transformer architecture and pathwise-gradient training through the inventory dynamics. Across problem sizes up to 1,024 inventory items, OR-Transformer increasingly outperforms learning-based and rolling-horizon MILP baselines as scale grows. It also reduces online decision-making time by over 4 million times relative to MILP solvers, enabling real-time, large-scale deep RL in supply chain operations.

</details>


### [205] [Refining Heuristic-Based Bitcoin Address Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01942)
*Hugo Schnoering,Roman Bresson,Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用图神经网络对比嵌入来精细化启发式比特币地址聚类的方法，通过层次聚类实现更细粒度的分析，并对可疑合并提供定量判定标准；同时发布了包含大量聚类的公开比特币交易图数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币的伪匿名性使用户级活动分析困难重重，因为一个用户可能控制多个地址。现有基于启发式的方法试图识别属于同一用户的地址，但往往产生模块度有限的扁平聚类，且容易出错（如将不同用户合并）。

Method: 提出一种以图神经网络产生的对比嵌入为基础来精炼启发式聚类的方法。包括：(i) 发布包含大量聚类的公开比特币交易图数据集；(ii) 提出学习与启发式规则一致的地址嵌入的方法论，并给出理论指导直觉；(iii) 通过层次聚类实现对启发式聚类的更细粒度分析，并提供定量判据来标记可疑的合并。

Result: 该方法以图神经网络对比嵌入为聚类基础，能够精炼启发式获得的聚类；层次聚类为启发式聚类提供更细粒度的分析，且提供一个定量判据用于标识可疑的用户合并。

Conclusion: 通过将GNN对比嵌入与层次聚类相结合，本文能够细化基于启发式的比特币地址聚类，识别并标记可能将不同用户错误合并的可疑聚类，同时以公开数据集的形式为后续研究提供基础。

Abstract: Bitcoin's pseudonymous nature makes it challenging to analyze user-level activity, since a single user may control multiple identifiers (addresses). Existing heuristic-based methods attempt to identify addresses belonging to the same user, but they often produce flat cluster assignments with limited modularity and are prone to errors such as merging different users together. In this work, we propose a method for refining heuristic-obtained clusters by grounding our clustering on contrastive embeddings yielded by graph neural networks. Our contributions are threefold: (i) we release a publicly available dataset of Bitcoin transaction graphs containing a substantial number of clusters; (ii) we propose a methodology for learning address embeddings consistent with heuristics, and back it up with theoretical guiding intuitions; (iii) through hierarchical clustering, we enable a finer analysis of heuristic clusters and provide a quantitative criterion for flagging suspicious merges.

</details>


### [206] [On-Policy Distillation Meets Off-Policy GRPO: Training Compact Instruction-Following Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01947)
*Vignesh Prabhakar,Jialing Pan,Anil Babu Ankisettipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段的重排序器蒸馏框架：第一阶段用离策略GRPO和LLM评判反馈强化4B教师重排序器，第二阶段让1B学生从自身策略采样排序并用教师派生的软奖励进行在策略蒸馏，在分布偏移和全量评测上均显著超越离线KD与GKD等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重排序器蒸馏通过离线模仿教师在固定示例上的输出来训练学生，监督信号被限制在教师已观测的排序空间中，限制了学生的探索与泛化能力。作者旨在用强化学习视角重新审视蒸馏，将教师优化与学生蒸馏解耦为离策略与在策略两阶段，以提升紧凑学生模型在分布偏移下的表现。

Method: 提出两阶段框架。阶段一：在88K指令跟随示例上，利用LLM评判反馈对4B教师重排序器做离策略GRPO优化，增强其排序能力。阶段二：1B学生从自身策略采样排序结果，教师基于这些排序提供软奖励（软教师派生奖励），将学生探索与知识迁移耦合，实现在策略蒸馏。

Result: 在MAIR-11（11子集、869查询）上，学生达到0.7670 nDCG@6，比离线listwise KD高+4.6点；在MAIR-Full（126任务、9356查询）上取得最高任务宏平均估计：0.6808 nDCG@6、0.7865 MRR@6，并超越两个已发布的7B RL训练重排序器；同一阶段二训练流程一致提升三个架构不同的学生骨干；在9861查询验证集上达到0.7624 nDCG@6，兼顾质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 基于奖励的在策略蒸馏（在教师派生的软奖励下对学生自采样的排序进行优化）优于仅改变离线蒸馏目标或仅将在策略与教师分布匹配的做法，证明学生自采样与奖励耦合是蒸馏性能提升的关键，且该方法对多种学生架构具有可迁移性。

Abstract: Compact instruction-following rerankers are attractive for deployment, but conventional distillation pipelines typically train students by offline imitation of teacher outputs on a fixed set of examples, constraining supervision to the teacher's observed ranking space. We revisit reranker distillation through the lens of reinforcement learning.
  We propose a two-stage framework combining off-policy teacher optimization with on-policy student distillation. In Stage 1, a 4B teacher reranker is strengthened with off-policy GRPO using LLM-judge feedback on 88K instruction-following examples. In Stage 2, a compact 1B student samples rankings from its own policy and receives soft teacher-derived rewards on those rankings, coupling student exploration with knowledge transfer.
  Our strongest gains appear under distribution shift. On MAIR-11, the original 11-subset, 869-query evaluation, the proposed student reaches 0.7670 nDCG@6, outperforming offline listwise KD by +4.6 points. Controlled comparisons against offline pairwise RankNet KD and on-policy GKD show that neither changing the offline distillation objective nor moving teacher-distribution matching on-policy reproduces the performance of reward-based on-policy distillation over student-sampled rankings. The advantage persists on MAIR-Full: across all 126 tasks and 9,356 queries, the proposed method obtains the highest task-macro point estimates among the evaluated distillation variants, reaching 0.6808 nDCG@6 and 0.7865 MRR@6. It also exceeds two released 7B RL-trained rerankers on the comparable MAIR-11 evaluation, while the same Stage 2 training procedure consistently improves three architecturally distinct alternative student backbones.
  On the 9,861-query validation benchmark, the resulting 1B reranker achieves 0.7624 nDCG@6 while providing a favorable quality-efficiency tradeoff relative to larger alternatives.

</details>


### [207] [Convergence Theory of Knowledge Distillation in Asynchronous P2P Gossip Learning Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01952)
*Lucas Qingyang Fang,Tiyao Liu,Jinhao Jing,Zeji Li,Kaijie Chen,Harikrishna Kuttivelil,Katia Obraczka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为完全去中心化、异步的点对点知识蒸馏（KD）提供了首个收敛性理论：通过将共识从参数空间转移到函数（输出）空间，把KD事件建模为logit空间上的几何收缩算子，证明了时间平均的函数平稳性和函数空间分歧以O(1/(ηT))速率收敛到O(η)+O(B_f^2)+O(ζ_f^2)邻域；实验表明KD可将函数分歧压缩40-61倍，且孤立训练无法做到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化无服务器学习日益连接不同架构的设备，而标准工具去中心化SGD因参数数量不同无法平均而失效。知识蒸馏通过交换软预测而非权重绕过了这一障碍，但完全去中心化、异步P2P知识蒸馏的收敛理论此前是缺失的。

Method: 将共识从参数空间重定位到函数（输出）空间：把KD事件刻画为对等节点预测分布在logit空间上的几何收缩算子，并在参考测度上的预测希尔伯特空间中进行理论分析。在标准光滑性/方差假设以及两个可实现性假设（一个衔接参数SGD与函数步，一个控制受限任务/KD对齐）下推导收敛界，并通过函数平稳性诊断、步长扫描等方式进行实验验证。

Result: 时间平均的函数平稳性和函数空间分歧以O(1/(ηT))速率收敛到O(η)+O(B_f^2)+O(ζ_f^2)邻域，其中B_f为任务最优点到对等节点可达类别的距离，ζ_f衡量持续存在的局部任务异质性。实验中，在均匀、宽度异构和混合家族网络下，KD将函数分歧压缩40-61倍，而孤立训练无法实现；采样平稳性诊断在共享骨架主运行中呈现晚期瞬态指数0.99-1.90，四点点步长扫描呈现预测的瞬态-邻域权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了去中心化异步P2P知识蒸馏收敛理论的空白，证明即使模型架构不可兼容，函数空间中的蒸馏仍能实现共识与收敛，为异构设备上的去中心化学习提供了理论保证与实证验证。

Abstract: Decentralized, serverless learning increasingly connects devices running different architectures, where the standard tool, decentralized SGD, is undefined as models with different parameter counts cannot be averaged. Knowledge distillation (KD) exchanges soft predictions rather than weights and sidesteps this obstacle, yet convergence theory for fully decentralized, asynchronous peer-to-peer (P2P) KD is lacking. We provide one, relocating consensus from parameter space to function (output) space: a KD event is a geometric contraction operator in logit space on the peers' predictive distributions, which we analyse in the Hilbert space of predictions on a reference measure. Under standard smoothness/variance assumptions and two realizability assumptions, one bridging parameter SGD to the functional step and one controlling restricted task/KD alignment, the time-averaged functional stationarity and function-space disagreement converge at rate $O(1/(ηT))$ to an $O(η)+O(B_f^2)+O(ζ_f^2)$ neighbourhood. Here $B_f$ is the distance from the task optimum to the peers' reachable classes and $ζ_f$ measures persistent local-task heterogeneity. Across homogeneous, width-heterogeneous, and mixed-family networks of the experiments, KD contracts function disagreement by $40-61\times$, while isolated training does not. The sampled stationarity diagnostic has late transient exponents $0.99-1.90$ on the shared-skeleton main runs, and the four-point step-size sweep exhibits the predicted transient: neighbourhood tradeoff.

</details>


### [208] [FlashKAN: B-Spline KANs via Truncated Power Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01956)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashKAN 将 KAN 中可学习的均匀三次 B 样条激活从低效的 Cox-de Boor 递归求值改写为截断幂级数形式（统一的 (x)_+^3 项），并通过 torch.compile 融合为单个 GPU 内核、有界坐标稳定化防止灾难性抵消，最终以 pip install flashkan 的即插即用开源包形式发布，显著加速前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 KAN 采用 Cox-de Boor 递归对 k 次 B 样条激活进行求值，需要 k 次顺序遍历，前向传播超过 90% 的时间被消耗在此，成为性能瓶颈，亟需更高效的求值方案。

Method: 利用逼近论中的截断幂级数形式，将每个均匀三次 B 样条表示为五个在移位节点处的 (x)_+^3 项之和，从而完全去除递归、区间查找和 scatter-gather 操作；配合 torch.compile 将运算融合为单个 GPU 内核，并通过将有界归一化输入限制在 [0, k+1] 来稳定计算，避免灾难性抵消。

Result: 实现了消除全部递归与分散/聚集开销的单一 GPU 核融合实现，并提供生产级、开源的即插即用包（pip install flashkan），可直接替代现有 KAN 层。

Conclusion: FlashKAN 通过经典逼近论结果与工程化内核融合，在保持与现有 KAN 层兼容的前提下有效解决了 B 样条激活求值的效率问题，为 KAN 的规模化应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) place learnable B-spline activations on network edges rather than fixed activations on nodes. The standard Cox-de Boor recursion evaluates these activations through k sequential passes for degree-k splines, consuming over 90% of forward-pass time. FlashKAN replaces this recursion with the truncated power form, a classical result from approximation theory that expresses each uniform cubic B-spline as five (x)_+^3 terms at shifted knot positions. This paper makes three contributions: (1) a torch.compile-fused implementation that collapses these operations into a single GPU kernel, eliminating all recursion, span lookup, and scatter-gather operations; (2) a bounded-coordinate stabilization that clamps the normalized input to [0, k+1], preventing the catastrophic cancellation that historically motivated the Cox-de Boor recursion; and (3) a production-ready, open-source package (pip install flashkan) that serves as a drop-in replacement for existing KAN layers.

</details>


### [209] [A Unified Particle Filter LSTM for Data-Driven Process Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01967)
*Parvin Malekzadeh,Opher Baron,Dmitry Krass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种统一粒子滤波LSTM（Unified PF-LSTM），通过维护并更新一组加权循环状态假设来处理事件日志中的部分可观测过程状态，用于数据驱动的业务流程仿真，在三个真实急诊数据集上优于现有数据驱动基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件日志仅提供底层过程的局部视图（通常只记录活动完成时间，缺乏服务开始时间），因此相同的观测过程历史可能对应多个潜在的过程状态。而标准循环模型将每个过程前缀压缩为单一确定性的循环状态，无法刻画这种多假设/多模态的不确定性，从而限制了仿真生成的准确性。

Method: 提出Unified PF-LSTM框架：维护并通过粒子滤波顺序更新一组加权循环状态假设（粒子信念）；使用加权均值及基于矩生成函数学习到的特征对粒子信念进行汇总；依据该表示预测下一活动的类别分布以及当前活动逗留时间的条件分位数；整个框架基于事件日志端到端训练。

Result: 在三个真实世界急诊科数据集上的实验表明，所提框架在复现路由、持续时间及系统级行为方面一致优于所考虑的数据驱动基线；在复杂过程动态仅被部分反映于事件日志的设置中，性能提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模循环状态的不确定性（粒子信念）而非仅用单一确定性状态，Unified PF-LSTM能够更准确地从事件日志中进行数据驱动的过程仿真，尤其在过程动态部分可观测的场景下带来显著改进。

Abstract: Data-driven process simulation aims to generate realistic case trajectories from historical event logs without requiring an explicitly specified model of the underlying dynamics. Deep sequence models can capture complex temporal dependencies through next-activity probabilities and conditional time distributions. However, event logs provide only a partial view of the underlying process state, often recording activity completions without the corresponding service-start times. Consequently, the same observed process history may be consistent with multiple plausible latent process conditions, whereas standard recurrent models compress each process prefix into a single deterministic recurrent state. We propose a Unified Particle Filter LSTM (Unified PF-LSTM) that maintains and sequentially updates a weighted set of recurrent-state hypotheses. We summarize this particle belief using its weighted mean and learned features based on the moment-generating function. The resulting representation is used to predict a categorical distribution over the next activity and conditional quantiles of the current activity's sojourn time. The framework is trained end-to-end from event-log data and evaluated on three real-world emergency department datasets. The results show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms the considered data-driven baselines in reproducing routing, duration, and system-level behavior across all datasets, with particularly strong gains in settings where complex process dynamics are only partially reflected in the available event logs.

</details>


### [210] [CAHR-Net: Condition-Adaptive Hysteresis Reconstruction for Compact and Interpretable Magnetic Core Loss Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01991)
*Chunye Gong,Cong Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAHR-Net，一种条件自适应磁滞重建网络，通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）将频率、温度、波形统计等工况条件注入中间磁滞重建表征，而非仅作用于终端标量，从而在保持"磁通密度波形→磁场重建→磁滞回线面积积分→铁损估计"可解释链路的同时，在MagNet A-E材料测试集上以仅1874个参数取得平均p95相对误差6.89%的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁芯损耗源于磁滞回线：每周期的能耗等于回线面积，而频率、温度和波形形状通过重塑回线几何来设定损耗。现有模型大多让这些工况只作用于终端标量——经验公式将其折入拟合指数，数据驱动模型将其附加到编码特征——导致没有任何中间磁滞表征可供工况重塑。本文主张应在工况物理上真正起作用的位置（中间重建表征）注入条件。

Method: 提出CAHR-Net条件自适应磁滞重建网络：保留从磁通密度波形到磁场重建、回线面积积分、功率损耗估计的可解释链路，并用特征级线性调制（FiLM）将频率、温度和波形统计注入中间重建表征；同时报告与之匹配的大批量训练协议，基于AdamW、余弦调度及分阶段的先重建后损耗目标函数，因为调制通路只有在该训练流程下才起作用。

Result: 在MagNet最终A-E材料协议上，CAHR-Net以1874个参数取得平均p95相对误差6.89%，为所有对比方法中最低；最差材料的p95也低于最强黑盒方案，且参数量约为其1/48；它将物理重建骨干网络的平均p95从7.47%降至6.89%，并将最困难材料D的p95从16.40%降至14.87%。

Conclusion: 消融实验和条件切片分析表明，性能提升归因于物理回线重建、结构化条件调制与匹配的优化轨迹三者的耦合，验证了在工况物理作用位置注入条件、而非仅在终端叠加条件的有效性。

Abstract: Magnetic core loss originates in the hysteresis loop: the energy dissipated per excitation cycle equals the loop area, and frequency, temperature, and waveform shape set the loss by reshaping the loop geometry. Most existing models let these conditions act only on a terminal scalar - empirical equations fold them into fitted exponents, and data-driven predictors append them to encoded features - so no intermediate hysteresis representation remains for the conditions to reshape. This paper proposes CAHR-Net, a condition-adaptive hysteresis reconstruction network that injects the operating conditions where they physically act. It preserves the interpretable chain from flux density waveform to magnetic field reconstruction, loop-area integration, and power loss estimation, and uses feature-wise linear modulation to inject frequency, temperature, and waveform statistics into the intermediate reconstruction representation. A matched large-batch training protocol based on AdamW, cosine scheduling, and a staged reconstruction-to-power-loss objective is also reported, because the modulation pathway takes effect only within it. On the MagNet final A-E material protocol, CAHR-Net attains an average p95 relative error of 6.89% with only 1874 parameters, the lowest among all compared methods, together with a lower worst-material p95 than the strongest black-box solution at about 48x fewer parameters; it reduces the average p95 of the physical reconstruction backbone from 7.47% to 6.89% and the p95 of material D, the most difficult material, from 16.40% to 14.87%. Ablation and condition-slice analyses attribute the improvement to the coupling of physical loop reconstruction, structured condition modulation, and the matched optimization trajectory.

</details>


### [211] [Train What You Deploy: Closing the MLP Reachability Gap in Low-Rank Clone Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02006)
*Wenhui Chen,Zhifeng Li,Jie Zhou,Navan Preet Singh,Madalina Ciobanu,Chenghua Wang,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个核心原则"训练你所部署的"（train what you deploy），用于修正权重继承蒸馏器中的"容量搁浅"问题。现有 SOTA 方法 Low-Rank Clone (LRC) 在推理时部署全宽度的学生 MLP，但其训练却被限制在教师诱导的低秩子空间内，导致部署矩阵中 62.5-81.4% 的独立线性自由度在推理时付出代价却永远无法被训练。作者在不改变部署形状、参数量或推理 FLOPs 的前提下，将训练目标扩展到整个部署矩阵，通过两个可合并的实现（Dense-LRC 与 CORE-LRC）在三个教师模型上相对匹配预算的 plain-LRC 基线分别取得 +2.36/+2.71/+10.45 的 Avg9 提升，最大收益来自最宽的 Qwen 教师（50% 的 token 效率提升）；受控实验支持增益来源是扩大的可达权重集而非额外参数。此外，约 10B 蒸馏 token 加短 SFT 后，半参数的 1.5B 学生即可在 9 任务宏平均上匹配其约 9T token 的教师，2.7B 学生以约 900 倍少的压缩 token 超越 Meta 官方压缩结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩学生的部署形状与训练可达的权重族这两者并不必然一致：LRC 这类权重继承蒸馏器部署全宽学生 MLP，却将训练约束在教师诱导的低秩切片上（每层约 62.5-81.4% 的线性自由度在推理中被激活、却根本无法被训练追上），造成"已付费却不可训练"的搁浅容量。作者希望在不改动部署成本的前提下回收这部分容量。

Method: 遵从"训练你所部署的"原则：从完全相同的 LRC warm start 出发，将训练对象改为完整的部署矩阵。具体给出两个可合并的实现 Dense-LRC 与 CORE-LRC，两者训练过程不同但都坍缩为同一个部署权重，因此不改变部署形状、部署参数量与推理 FLOPs；通过将训练优化器与部署权重解耦来扩大训练可达集。

Result: 相对匹配预算的 plain-LRC 基线，在 Llama3.2-3B、Llama3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-3B 三个教师上 Avg9 分别提升 +2.36/+2.71/+10.45（取每位教师更强的实现）；最宽的 Qwen 教师上收益最大，以 10B token 达到原配方约 20B token 的精度（2 倍 token 效率），同一谱系的严格受控臂仍有 +6.39。控制实验表明增益源于扩大的可达集而非新增参数或配方。约 10B 蒸馏 token + 短 SFT 后，半参数的 1.5B 学生在 9 任务宏平均上匹配约 9T token 的教师（评估噪声内，MMLU 仍有残余差距）；2.7B 学生以约 900 倍少的压缩 token 超越 Meta 官方对 Llama3.1-8B 的压缩结果（token 数量对比基于不完全匹配的配方，非计算量声明）。全部结果为 LRC 骨干上的单种子运行。

Conclusion: 将训练目标对齐到实际部署的完整矩阵（而非教师诱导的低秩切片）能有效回收被搁浅的容量，带来稳定的下游增益，并显著提升 token 效率；该原则不增加任何部署成本，是权重继承蒸馏中简单而有效的修正。

Abstract: A compressed student has two shapes that need not agree: the weight it deploys at inference and the weight family its training can reach. We show that a state-of-the-art weight-inheritance distiller, Low-Rank Clone (LRC), deploys a full-width student MLP but ties training to a teacher-induced slice, leaving 62.5-81.4% of each deployed matrix's independent linear degrees of freedom unreachable-paid for at inference, never trainable. Our principle is one line: train what you deploy. From the identical LRC warm start, we make the training object the entire deployed matrix, with no change in deployed shape, deployed parameter count, or inference FLOPs, via two mergeable realizations (Dense-LRC and CORE-LRC) that both collapse to one deployed weight. This recovers stranded capacity: taking the stronger realization per teacher, +2.36/+2.71/+10.45 Avg9 over matched-budget plain-LRC baselines across three teachers (Llama3.2-3B, Llama3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-3B), with the largest gain on the widest teacher (Qwen), where it reaches the original recipe's approx. 20B-token accuracy at 10B tokens (2x token efficiency); there the strictly same-lineage arm still recovers +6.39, the fully controlled figure. Controls strongly support attributing the gain to the enlarged reachable set, rather than to added parameters or the recipe. From approx. 10B distillation tokens plus a short SFT, a half-parameter 1.5B student matches its approx. 9T-token teacher's 9-task macro-average, within evaluation noise and with a residual MMLU deficit, and a 2.7B student beats Meta's own official compression of Llama3.1-8B at ~900x fewer compression tokens (a token count under unmatched recipes, not a compute claim). All results are from single-seed runs on the LRC backbone.

</details>


### [212] [Source-Free Class Relearning: Diagnosing Forgetting in Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02018)
*Zahra Dehghani,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究类别遗忘（class unlearning）后的可恢复性审计问题，提出了一种严格无源（source-free）的白盒审计方法 SFRA：仅利用未学习模型，通过合成探针嵌入并基于模型置信度筛选高置信保留探针与低置信边界探针（重标注为遗忘类），单步梯度更新分类头即可评测遗忘类是否可被恢复。作者还提出 Relearning Score (RS) 指标，在 CIFAR-10/100、TinyImageNet 及多种架构上表明多项未学习方法存在显著的无源可恢复性，部分方法甚至超过重训练参照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别遗忘评估仅以遗忘准确率低为指标，但这并不代表类别结构已被真正擦除——近似未学习方法可能只改变了决策边界，而表征中仍保留可恢复的类别结构。已有恢复遗忘类的工作依赖真实遗忘/保留样本、辅助数据或参考检查点，缺乏严格无源条件下的恢复性审计手段。作者希望回答：仅凭未学习模型，能否通过更新分类头重新恢复遗忘类，从而揭示未学习方法的真实擦除程度。

Method: 首先建立理论分析：证明存在一个充分的对齐条件，在该条件下对合成探针集执行单步梯度更新即可增大遗忘类的期望 logit 间隔（margin）。基于此提出白盒 Source-Free Relearning Audit (SFRA)：在表征空间中生成候选嵌入，利用模型引导的置信度过滤构造两类探针——高置信度的保留类探针，以及低置信度、位于决策边界附近并被重标注为遗忘类的探针；随后用这些探针以单次梯度步更新分类头以尝试恢复遗忘类。默认采用高斯采样生成候选嵌入、Softmax 置信度作为筛选准则，并通过消融实验验证可恢复性不依赖于这些特定选择。为量化可恢复性，提出 Relearning Score (RS)，联合衡量遗忘类恢复程度与保留类精度保持，并以类匹配的 ΔRS（相对重训练参照）作为报告指标。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet 上，配合 ResNet-18、ViT-B/16、Swin-T 的实验表明：多种未学习方法在严格无源设置下表现出显著的可恢复性；且对于其中一部分方法，其可恢复性甚至超过了类匹配的重训练参照（ΔRS 为正）。消融实验还表明高斯采样与 Softmax 置信度之外的替代分布与不确定性准则同样能检测到可恢复性，说明该方法具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 遗忘准确率低并不能证明类别结构已被彻底擦除；通过仅基于未学习模型的白盒审计（SFRA）即可在单步分类头更新后恢复遗忘类，这为评估未学习方法提供了更严格的“擦除程度”基准。研究同时揭示了部分常用未学习方法的脆弱性——其可恢复性甚至超过重训练参照，提示实际部署中需要更稳健的遗忘策略与审计手段。

Abstract: Class unlearning aims to remove a model's ability to recognize designated forget classes while preserving performance on retain classes. However, low forget accuracy after unlearning does not necessarily mean the class structure has been erased. Approximate unlearning methods can alter classifier decision boundaries while leaving recoverable structure in the representation. Prior work has shown that forget classes can be recovered, but existing approaches require real forget or retain samples, auxiliary data, or reference checkpoints. We study class relearning in a strictly source-free setting, asking whether a forget class can be recovered through a classifier-head update using only the unlearned model. Our approach rests on a theoretical analysis establishing a sufficient alignment condition under which a single gradient step on a synthetic probe set increases the expected logit margin of the forget class. Building on this, we propose a white-box Source-Free Relearning Audit (SFRA), which generates candidate embeddings in representation space and uses model-guided confidence filtering to construct high-confidence retain probes and low-confidence boundary-adjacent probes that are relabelled as the forget class. Gaussian sampling and Softmax confidence are used by default, while ablations with alternative proposal distributions and uncertainty criteria show that recoverability is not specific to these choices. To quantify recoverability, we introduce the Relearning Score (RS), which jointly measures forget-class recovery and retain-accuracy preservation, and report class-matched $Δ$RS relative to a retrained reference. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and TinyImageNet with ResNet-18, ViT-B/16, and Swin-T show that several unlearning methods exhibit substantial source-free recoverability, and that for a subset of methods this recoverability exceeds the matched retrained reference.

</details>


### [213] [Act More, Decide Less: Skill-Guided Adaptive Action Chunking for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02042)
*Yanting Yang,Can Jin,Jinman Zhao,Jiahao Wu,Yang Zhou,Zhepeng Wang,Zhendong Wang,Mu Zhou,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPACE方法，通过从成功轨迹诱导的程序化技能中蒸馏块边界监督，使LLM智能体能够输出变长动作块，在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上显著提升成功率并大幅减少LLM决策轮数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct式协议每轮LLM只执行一个原始动作，虽支持频繁重规划，但在长程任务中大量轮次耗费在常规动作序列上，效率低下。让智能体输出变长动作块是自然替代方案，但朴素强化学习训练会失败：策略要么坍缩为单动作行为，要么过度承诺过长序列，二者共同根因是无法学习块的边界。

Method: SPACE从成功轨迹中诱导两级程序化技能，将子技能边界作为直接的块边界监督；随后通过混合on-/off-policy优化结合块感知的信用分配，把这种时间结构蒸馏到"原始-块"策略中。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上，SPACE相比各设置下最强基线将成功率提升7.0%-31.3%，同时将平均LLM决策轮数最多减少78.9%。

Conclusion: 块边界监督是训练变长动作块策略的关键；借助轨迹诱导的程序化技能蒸馏可以有效解决朴素RL失败的问题，在提升长程交互任务成功率的同时大幅降低推理开销。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents for long-horizon interactive tasks typically follow a ReAct-style protocol, issuing one primitive action per LLM round. While this enables frequent replanning, it is inefficient for long-horizon tasks where many rounds are spent on routine action sequences. A natural alternative is to let the agent emit variable-length action chunks. However, naively training such policies with standard reinforcement learning fails: the agent either collapses to single-action behavior or over-commits to excessively long sequences. Both failures share a common root cause: the inability to learn chunk boundaries. We propose SPACE, which addresses this challenge by distilling chunk-boundary supervision from trajectory-induced programmatic skills. We induce two-level programmatic skills from successful trajectories, where subskill boundaries serve as direct chunk-boundary supervision. This temporal structure is then distilled into a primitive-chunk policy via hybrid on-/off-policy optimization with chunk-aware credit assignment. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that SPACE improves success rates by 7.0%-31.3% over the strongest baseline in each setting while reducing average LLM decision rounds by up to 78.9%.

</details>


### [214] [The Dynamics of Continuous Mixture Collapse in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02049)
*Ali Backour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预训练语言模型为何无法保持基于隐状态（潜在状态）推理方法所使用的连续混合表示（token 嵌入的加权混合）。通过理论分析与多模型受控实验，作者识别出三个独立且不同的失败来源：架构固有几何失真（且训练会放大）、softmax 读出与自回归反馈构成的动力学系统导致的放大/收缩不稳定性，以及多成分混合时需要依赖上下文校正且所需维度随成分数增长。理论与实验在阈值附近高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的隐状态推理方法用连续状态（如 token 嵌入的加权混合）替代离散中间 token，以同时保留多个可能的推理方向。但预训练语言模型往往无法保持这些混合表示，导致此类方法失效；此前缺乏对“为何失效”的系统理解，本文旨在从机理上弄清这些失败的本质来源。

Method: 采用理论分析与受控实证研究相结合的方式：首先在数学上刻画 transformer 架构对混合几何的扭曲，并分析训练放大作用；然后将 softmax 读出与自回归反馈建模为一个动力学系统，推导混合成分之间放大与收缩转变的理论阈值；最后在多种预训练模型上进行受控实验，验证理论预测，并将分析推广到多成分混合情形，研究精确保持所需校正的维度规模。

Result: 识别出三个独立、不同的失败来源：(1) transformer 架构本身会扭曲混合几何，且训练显著放大该效应；(2) 即使在完美的线性传输下，softmax 读出与自回归反馈组成的动力学系统也会将微小差异放大至单一成分支配，或将不同混合收缩至不可区分，失败由此产生；(3) 对多成分混合，精确保持通常需要依赖上下文的校正，其所需维度可随成分数量增长。观察到收缩与放大之间的转变点接近理论推导阈值，且预训练模型的自回归展开主要落在放大侧。

Conclusion: 预训练语言模型无法保留连续混合状态并非单一原因所致，而是源于架构几何失真（并被训练放大）、读出-反馈动力学不稳定性以及多成分混合的表示维度瓶颈这三类相互独立的机制。理论与实验的定量一致性表明这些机制是内在且可预测的，为设计更鲁棒的隐状态推理方法提供了理论依据与边界条件。

Abstract: LLMs latent-state reasoning methods replace discrete intermediate tokens with continuous states, such as weighted mixtures of token embeddings, to retain multiple possible reasoning directions rather than committing to one. Yet pretrained language models often fail to preserve these mixtures. We study why through a combination of theoretical analysis and controlled empirical investigations on a variety of models. We identify three independent, distinct sources of failure. First, transformer architectures already distort mixture geometry, and training substantially amplifies this effect. Moreover, the failure can occur even if the model transports mixtures perfectly linearly: the softmax readout and autoregressive feedback form a dynamical system that either amplifies small differences until one component of the mixture dominates or contracts different mixtures until they become indistinguishable. We verify this theoretical prediction empirically: the observed transition between contraction and amplification occurs near the theoretical threshold derived by our analysis, and pretrained-model rollouts lie predominantly on the amplifying side. Finally, we generalize to mixtures of many components and show that exact preservation generally requires context-dependent correction, whose required dimensionality can grow with the number of components.

</details>


### [215] [DynG-Diff: A State-Aware Dynamic Guidance Diffusion Framework for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02068)
*Zhente Zhang,Zhengwei Ni,Wei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DynG-Diff，一种面向概率多变量时间序列预测的变量敏感动态引导扩散框架。它采用两阶段分离训练的无条件扩散主干建模联合分布，并通过轻量级状态感知策略网络根据实时噪声状态与单步去噪估计输出动态引导强度矩阵，将该权重数学化为观测分布的局部精度，从而在推理时精确引导高置信度变量并滤除异常噪声干扰。在真实基准上的实验表明其达到与 SOTA 条件扩散模型相当的概率预测性能，且在严重观测损坏下具有更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的多变量时间序列预测方法依赖任务特定的条件范式，缺乏灵活性，并且难以应对"信息异质性"问题——即不同变量之间噪声水平和演化模式差异显著，导致预测精度和鲁棒性受限。

Method: DynG-Diff 包含三个核心设计：(1) 两阶段分离训练策略，使用无条件扩散主干建模多变量时间序列的联合分布；(2) 轻量级状态感知策略网络，从实时噪声状态和单步去噪估计中自适应推断变量可靠性，并输出动态引导强度矩阵；(3) 将该动态权重数学形式化为观测分布的局部精度，使模型在推理时对高置信度变量进行精确引导，同时滤除异常噪声的干扰。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，DynG-Diff 与最先进的条件扩散模型相比取得了具有竞争力的概率预测性能，并在严重观测损坏情况下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DynG-Diff 通过动态、变量敏感的引导机制有效解决了多变量时间序列预测中的信息异质性问题，在保持预测精度的同时显著提升了抗噪鲁棒性，为概率时间序列预测提供了一种更灵活且可扩展的扩散建模方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic multivariate time series (MTS) forecasting is crucial for modeling complex dynamical systems. However, existing diffusion-based methods rely on task-specific conditional paradigms that lack flexibility and struggle with inherent "information heterogeneity"--the significantly varying noise levels and evolutionary patterns across variables. To address this, we propose DynG-Diff, a variable-sensitive dynamic guidance diffusion framework for probabilistic multivariate time-series forecasting: (1) DynG-Diff adopts a two-stage separated training strategy and uses an unconditional diffusion backbone to model the joint distribution of multivariate time series. (2) DynG-Diff introduces a lightweight state-aware policy network that adaptively infers variable reliability from real-time noisy states and one-step denoising estimates, outputting a dynamic guidance strength matrix. (3) DynG-Diff mathematically formulates this dynamic weight as the local precision of the observation distribution, enabling precise guidance for high-confidence variables during inference while filtering out interference from anomalous noise. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate competitive probabilistic forecasting performance against state-of-the-art conditional diffusion models and improved robustness under severe observation corruption.The implementation code is available at: https://github.com/TT-20011031/DynG-Diff

</details>


### [216] [XMerge: Cross-Axis Selection and Reconstructive Layer Merging for LLM Depth Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02083)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: XMerge 是一种无标签、无需端到端微调的后训练深度压缩方法，通过跨轴选择（cross-axis selection）定位低相对幅度/角隐藏状态变化的层块，再用局部边界重建（local boundary reconstruction）让相邻幸存块拟合原两块的输出，从而在不改变架构、不增加推理参数的前提下移除完整 Transformer 层。实验证明在最激进的 k=4 层删除下其优势最大，在多个基准和基座上优于现有深度压缩方法且从不崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接移除完整 Transformer 层虽能保留标准服务架构，但现有深度压缩方法在质量上损失显著，且损失在不同模型间波动不可预测；需要一种代价可控、质量稳定的层压缩后训练方法。

Method: 提出 XMerge，包含两个组件：(1) 跨轴选择——按相对幅度与角度的隐藏状态变化两个轴识别低影响的层块；(2) 局部边界重建——重新拟合相邻的幸存块以匹配原始两块的输出。全程不使用任务标签、不做端到端微调、不引入架构改动或额外的推理时参数。

Result: 在七个 Llama/Qwen 基座（0.5B-8B）、五个已发布基线和三种层删除级别上，最激进的 k=4 设置下优势最大：在 CORE（22 任务聚合）六/七基座排名第一，MMLU 上六/七（两者同时满足五/七），且避免了多个竞争算子的困惑度大幅上升。任务级 bootstrap 显示三个最大 CORE 差距的 95% 置信区间不含零，其余与平局一致；在 14 个（模型, 场景）单元中是唯一从不崩溃的算子，且在首个校准探针上校准最佳。消融表明局部重建贡献了大部分增益，跨轴融合在两轴不一致时发挥作用。额外构建成本约需数万请求的每 token 解码节省来回收。

Conclusion: XMerge 是一种高质量、稳健、即插即用的层压缩方案，在激进压缩场景下优势最明显，且唯一地从不发生质量崩溃，兼具可校准性与实际部署成本可行性，可作为 Transformer 深度压缩的可靠默认选择。

Abstract: Removing complete transformer layers preserves a standard serving architecture, but existing depth-compression methods can lose substantial quality, and the loss varies unpredictably across models. We introduce XMerge, a post-training method with two components. Cross-axis selection identifies a block with low relative-magnitude and angular hidden-state change, and local boundary reconstruction re-fits the adjacent surviving block to match the original two-block output. XMerge uses no task labels or end-to-end fine-tuning, and it introduces neither architectural changes nor additional inference-time parameters. Across seven Llama and Qwen backbones (0.5B-8B), five published baselines, and three layer-reduction levels, its advantage over baselines is largest at the most aggressive removal: at k=4 it ranks first on six of seven backbones on CORE (a 22-task aggregate) and, separately, on six of seven on MMLU (five of seven on both at once), while avoiding the large perplexity increases of several competing operators. In a task-level bootstrap, the 95% confidence intervals for the three largest CORE margins exclude zero; the remaining margins are consistent with ties. Across the 14 (model, regime) cells it is also the only evaluated operator that never collapses, ranking top-2 in both zero-shot and in-context regimes; on a first calibration probe (one backbone) it is the best-calibrated operator. Ablations show that local reconstruction provides most of the gain, while cross-axis fusion helps when the two selection axes disagree. The additional construction cost is recovered through per-token decode savings after roughly tens of thousands of requests.

</details>


### [217] [TC-Next: Zero-Shot Multimodal Cyclone Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02085)
*Zhe Wang,Sijie Chen,Yiming Luo,Daehyun Kim,Chien-Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TC-Next 是一个多模态深度学习模型，结合基础模型生成的大气运动学和热力学预报场与 GridSat 红外卫星图像，用于 6-24 小时热带气旋路径与强度预报；仅在 GraphCast 西太平洋预报场上训练，即可显著优于传统规则追踪器 TempestExtremes，且无需重训练即可迁移至 Pangu-Weather、IFR HRES，并在 WeatherNext Cyclones 上零样本取得更优或相当的强度与路径误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带气旋的路径与强度短期预报（6-24 小时）至关重要，但传统基于规则的追踪器（如 TempestExtremes）性能有限。作者旨在利用基础模型（foundation model）丰富的通用大气预报场，并结合卫星红外影像这一多模态信息，训练一个专门的气旋追踪与强度预报模型，以克服传统方法的局限。

Method: 构建多模态深度学习模型 TC-Next，输入由基础模型（GraphCast）生成的通用大气运动学与热力学预报场（仅依赖通用变量），以及 GridSat 红外卫星图像；采用监督训练（仅在 GraphCast 西太平洋预报场上训练），并通过消融实验验证多模态融合的贡献。

Result: 在 GraphCast 上，TC-Next 相比 TempestExtremes 将路径误差降低 15%-44%、强度误差降低 3-6 倍；无需重训练即可迁移到 Pangu-Weather 与 IFS HRES 预报场并保持优势；在 2025 西太平洋季节的 WeatherNext Cyclones 上零样本应用，各超前时间强度误差更低，路径误差更低或相当；消融实验表明多模态在全部超前时间的追踪误差和更长超前时间的强度预测上均有提升。

Conclusion: TC-Next 证明了一种仅依赖通用大气变量的多模态方法可以超越传统规则追踪器，并具有跨基础模型（GraphCast、Pangu-Weather、IFS HRES、WeatherNext Cyclones）的良好泛化与零样本迁移能力，为热带气旋短期预报提供了一种通用且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: We present TropicalCycloneNext (TC-Next), a multimodal deep learning model that forecasts tropical cyclone track and intensity at $6$-$24$ h leads by leveraging a foundation model's forecast fields of atmospheric kinematic and thermodynamic fields and GridSat infrared satellite imagery. Trained only on GraphCast forecasts over the Western Pacific (WP), yet reliant only on generic atmospheric variables, TC-Next on GraphCast lowers track error by $15$-$44\%$ and intensity error by a factor of $3$-$6$ relative to a conventional, rule-based tracker, TempestExtremes; applied without retraining to the forecast fields of Pangu-Weather and IFS HRES, it stays ahead of TempestExtremes on both. Applied zero-shot to the generic weather fields of WeatherNext Cyclones on the 2025 WP season, TC-Next attains lower intensity error at every lead time, and lower or comparable track error, compared to that model's specialized direct tracker in a deterministic comparison. Our ablation studies show that our multimodal model is able to utilize the additional modality to improve performance in tracking errors at every lead time and in intensity prediction at longer lead times.

</details>


### [218] [Compositional Spectral Prompts for LLM-based Online Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02093)
*Seungyoon Choi,Hyunchul Kim,Jae-Gil Lee,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoSPOT 是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的在线时间序列预测框架。它冻结预训练 LLM 作为骨干预测器，仅通过频率域上的组合式频谱提示（compositional spectral prompts）进行在线适配，将未见模式表示为已学习频率基提示的新组合，从而解决现有基于记忆缓冲检索方法在长期适配和泛化到未见模式上的不足。实验表明其在扩展在线阶段和跨数据集分布漂移场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线时间序列预测（OTSF）需要应对序列的时序性与动态演化特性。现有研究主要依靠基于记忆缓冲的检索策略来适应非平稳环境，但作者观察到这类框架难以进行长期适应，并且无法泛化到未见过的模式，因此需要一种能高效在线适配并具备更强泛化能力的新方案。

Method: CoSPOT 采用预训练的 LLM 作为在线预测骨干，利用其强大的少样本能力；在线阶段保持 LLM 冻结，大幅减少需更新的参数。其核心是频率域组合式频谱提示：将时间序列分解为频率基，并根据各频率分量幅值组合对应的频谱基提示，使未见模式可通过已学到基提示的新组合来表示，从而引导模型把握输入的整体分布。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，CoSPOT 在具有挑战性的在线场景下（包括长时间在线阶段以及存在显著分布漂移的跨数据集设置）展现出优越性和实用性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CoSPOT 通过冻结 LLM 并引入基于频率基的组合式频谱提示，在显著减少在线阶段更新参数量的同时，实现了对未见模式的有效泛化与长期在线适应，为基于 LLM 的在线时间序列预测提供了可行且高效的框架。

Abstract: To address the sequential and evolving nature of time series, the Online Time Series Forecasting (OTSF) task has been extensively studied in multiple domains. Existing research focuses on adapting to non-stationary environments by employing memory buffer-based retrieval strategies. However, we observe that such frameworks struggle with long-term adaptation and fail to generalize to unseen patterns. To this end, we introduce CoSPOT, an LLM-based online time series forecasting framework that leverages a pre-trained LLM as the backbone online forecaster, motivated by its strong few-shot capabilities. For efficient online adaptation, CoSPOT keeps the LLM frozen and employs compositional spectral prompts grounded in frequency-domain bases to guide the model with the overall distribution of the input, thereby substantially reducing the number of parameters updated during the online phase. Specifically, CoSPOT decomposes time series into frequency bases and composes the corresponding spectral basis prompts according to their amplitudes, allowing unseen patterns to be represented as new combinations of learned basis prompts. Our extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority and practicality of CoSPOT across challenging online scenarios, including extended online phases and cross-dataset settings with substantial distribution shifts. Our code is available at https://github.com/seungyoon-Choi/CoSPOT.

</details>


### [219] [Federated LoRA Adaptation of BiomedCLIP Across Four International Chest X-Ray Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02101)
*Sanjaya Poudel,Nirajan Kunwor,Manish Dhakal,Debesh Jha,Sunil Kumar Gaire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在四个分布三大洲的胸部X光数据集上系统评估了联邦学习（FL）与低秩适配（LoRA）相结合的参数高效微调（PEFT），将BiomedCLIP的共享类别AUC均值从0.687提升至0.802，验证了FlexLoRA基于奇异值分解（SVD）的乘积空间聚合策略优于朴素因子平均与FedProx，并使联邦适配结果接近集中式训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许机构在不共享数据的前提下协同训练模型，LoRA通过只传输紧凑的低秩更新使其在规模上可行；生物医学影像正是这一组合的理想场景，因为患者数据受隐私法规约束，而各机构的扫描仪、协议和算力差异巨大。这种异构性引出了"联邦LoRA更新应如何聚合"的关键问题，尤其在多模态视觉-语言模型日益成为医学图像分析核心的背景下。

Method: 作者以BiomedCLIP为骨干，在四个公开队列（覆盖美国、越南、西班牙三大洲）的胸部X光分类任务上对联邦PEFT进行基准测试。比较了三种策略：未适配的预训练零样本骨干、单队列孤立训练、以及多种联邦聚合方案（含FedAvg朴素因子平均、FlexLoRA基于SVD的乘积空间聚合，以及加漂移校正的FedProx优化器）。

Result: 联邦LoRA适配使所有四个队列的共享类别AUC较未适配骨干显著提升（均值由0.687升至0.802），收益来自联邦适配而非预训练模型零样本能力；相对孤立单队列训练，联邦化增强了较弱队列并基本保持最强队列性能，接近集中式参考基线（0.812）。FlexLoRA的SVD乘积空间聚合是收益关键（朴素因子平均使平均AUC下降0.097），而FedProx在单次随机种子实验中相比FedAvg无明显优势，与LoRA低秩更新天然限制客户端漂移的结论一致。

Conclusion: 生物医学视觉-语言模型可以在不集中数据的前提下，跨异构、跨地域分布的机构实现协同适配，联邦LoRA（尤其是基于SVD的聚合策略）为受隐私约束的医学影像协作提供了一条可行且高效的路径。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) lets institutions train a shared model without exchanging data, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) makes this practical at scale by communicating only compact low-rank updates. Biomedical imaging is a compelling setting for this combination: patient data are archived behind privacy regulations, and institutions differ widely in scanners, protocols, and compute. Such heterogeneity raises the question of how federated LoRA updates should be aggregated, increasingly pressing as multimodal vision-language models become central to medical image analysis. We benchmark federated Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of BiomedCLIP for chest radiograph classification across four public cohorts on three continents (USA, Vietnam, Spain). Federated LoRA adaptation improves shared-class AUC on all four cohorts over the unadapted BiomedCLIP backbone (mean 0.687 to 0.802), showing that the gains come from federated adaptation rather than from the pretrained model's zero-shot ability. Relative to isolated single-cohort training, federation improves the weaker cohorts while largely preserving the strongest and approaches a centralized reference (0.812) that pools all data. The singular value decomposition (SVD)-based product-space aggregation introduced by FlexLoRA is essential to this gain (naive factor averaging drops mean AUC by 0.097), whereas a drift-correcting optimizer (FedProx) shows no benefit over FedAvg in our single-seed runs, consistent with LoRA's low-rank updates already limiting client drift. Biomedical vision-language models can thus be adapted collaboratively across heterogeneous, geographically distributed institutions without centralizing data.

</details>


### [220] [A Unified Rate-Distortion Perspective on Vector, Product, and Scalar Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02107)
*Xianghong Fang,Wenlong Mou,Yuan Yuan,Dehan Kong,Tim G. J. Rudner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的率-失真（rate-distortion）视角来理解现代离散视觉分词（visual tokenization）。它将量化视为有损压缩，以token数量和码本大小刻画标称定长编码率，以量化误差作为失真度量。在此框架下，作者回答了三个核心问题：最小化失真而非最大化码本利用率才是重建保真度的首要内在目标，并与STE梯度差异直接相关；建立了内在量化比较的两个公平性条件（控制潜在特征统计量、确保相同编码率）；并在这些条件下恢复了VQ-PQ-SQ失真层级，实证表明现代VQ方法失真最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散视觉分词技术主要由向量量化（VQ）、标量量化（SQ）和乘积量化（PQ）驱动，但缺乏一个统一的概念框架来理解这些量化方案之间的权衡（tradeoff），导致对量化器比较和评估存在歧义。

Method: 提出统一的率-失真框架：将量化视为有损压缩，用token数量与码本大小定义标称固定长度编码率，用量化误差作为失真。在此框架内：1）从理论和经验上证明最小化失真而非最大化码本利用率是重建保真的主要内在目标，并建立其与STE（straight-through estimator）引起的梯度差异的直接联系；2）提出内在量化比较的两个公平性条件——控制潜在特征统计量、强制相同编码率；3）在这些条件下恢复VQ-PQ-SQ的失真层级，并通过实验验证现代VQ方法达到最低失真。

Result: 建立了统一的率-失真框架，从理论上指明失真最小化（而非码本利用率）是量化保真度的核心目标；明确了量化公平比较所必需的统计量控制与同编码率两大条件；在该条件下恢复了VQ-PQ-SQ的固有失真层级，实证显示现代VQ方法失真最低。

Conclusion: 该工作为现代离散视觉分词提供了基础性的率-失真重构视角，消除了量化器评估中的歧义，并在固定比特率约束下为隔离内在量化有效性提供了一个受控框架。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokenization, predominantly driven by vector, scalar, and product quantization, lacks a unified conceptual framework for understanding quantization tradeoffs. In this paper, we propose a unified rate--distortion perspective on modern discrete visual tokenization. By viewing quantization as lossy compression, we characterize the nominal fixed-length coding rate through token count and codebook size, and quantization error as the distortion. Within this framework, we resolve three central questions. First, we theoretically and empirically show that minimizing distortion, rather than maximizing codebook utilization, is the primary intrinsic objective for reconstruction fidelity, with a direct connection to the STE-induced gradient discrepancy. Second, we establish two critical fairness conditions for intrinsic quantization comparison: controlling latent feature statistics and enforcing identical coding rates. Third, under these conditions, we recover the VQ--PQ--SQ distortion hierarchy in modern visual tokenization and show empirically that modern VQ methods achieve the lowest distortion. This work provides a foundational rate--distortion reframing of modern discrete visual tokenization, resolves ambiguities in quantizer evaluation, and provides a controlled framework for isolating intrinsic quantization effectiveness under fixed-rate constraints.

</details>


### [221] [A Computational Comparison of Fourier Spectral Differentiation and Spatial Automatic Differentiation in Periodic Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02110)
*Xilai Liang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在周期性物理空间PINN中对空间自动微分（AD）与傅里叶谱微分进行了受控对比实验。在固定神经网络表示、时间微分、优化器等所有其他因素仅改变空间微分方式的条件下，针对Allen-Cahn、Korteweg-de Vries和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程的五种方程-框架组合，傅里叶微分带来2.90倍至18.52倍的平均端到端训练加速，并将峰值GPU内存降低68.7%-94.1%，最终相对L2误差保持同一量级且无一致性精度优劣。结论是：在一维周期基准下，傅里叶谱微分以需要均匀结构化空间网格为代价，显著低于空间AD的训练时间和内存占用，同时保持相当的求解误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINN）通常使用自动微分（AD）计算偏微分方程残差中的空间导数，但当需要多个或高阶导数时，AD的计算和内存成本会显著增加。作者希望通过受控实验验证傅里叶谱微分能否作为更高效的空间微分替代方案。

Method: 采用配对实验设计：在每个实验中，神经网络表示、时间微分、优化器、采样流程和训练计划均保持不变，两种情形仅在空间微分方式上不同。傅里叶变体将网络输出在均匀周期网格上求值并变换到傅里叶空间，通过谱乘法获得各阶空间导数，且同一组傅里叶系数可在各导数阶次间复用。对比在标准PINN（Allen-Cahn、Korteweg-de Vries方程）和Causal PINN（Allen-Cahn、Korteweg-de Vries、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程）中展开。

Result: 在五种方程-框架设置中，傅里叶微分带来2.90倍至18.52倍的平均配对端到端训练加速，峰值GPU内存降低68.7%-94.1%；最终相对L2误差保持同一量级，两种微分方式在精度上无一致性优势。

Conclusion: 在一维周期基准问题中，傅里叶谱微分相比空间AD能大幅降低训练时间和内存占用，同时保持相当的求解误差，其代价是需要均匀的结构化空间网格。因此在该类问题上傅里叶谱微分是更具效率的空间微分选择。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) commonly evaluate the spatial derivatives appearing in partial differential equation residuals using automatic differentiation (AD), whose computational and memory costs can become substantial when multiple or high-order derivatives are required. We perform a controlled comparison of spatial AD and Fourier spectral differentiation in periodic physical-space PINNs. Within each paired experiment, the neural representation, temporal differentiation, optimizer, sampling procedure, and training schedule are held fixed, so that the two cases differ only in the spatial differentiation procedure. For the Fourier variant, network outputs are evaluated on a uniform periodic grid and transformed to Fourier space, where spatial derivatives are obtained through spectral multiplication and the same Fourier coefficients are reused across derivative orders. We compare the two procedures in standard PINNs for the Allen--Cahn and Korteweg--de Vries equations and in Causal PINNs for the Allen--Cahn, Korteweg--de Vries, and Kuramoto--Sivashinsky equations. Across these five equation--framework settings, Fourier differentiation yields mean paired end-to-end training speedups ranging from $2.90\times$ to $18.52\times$ and reduces peak allocated graphics processing unit (GPU) memory by $68.7\%$--$94.1\%$. The final relative $L_2$ errors remain of the same order, with neither differentiation procedure showing a consistent accuracy advantage. For the one-dimensional periodic benchmarks considered here, Fourier spectral differentiation therefore provides substantially lower training time and memory usage than spatial AD while retaining comparable solution error, at the cost of requiring a uniform structured spatial grid.

</details>


### [222] [Scalable Bayesian Optimization of Composite Functions for Image-Based Inverse Problems in Materials Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02126)
*Dasol Yoon,Poompol Buathong,Chia-Hao Lee,Yujia Zhang,David A. Muller,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SBOCF（可扩展复合函数贝叶斯优化），一种面向昂贵物理仿真逆向问题的仿真高效方法，用于从 PACBED 图像估计样品厚度与晶体失配角。通过利用图像匹配目标的已知复合结构，将 24,649 个像素级输出压缩为 11 个补丁级摘要加两个修正项，在 50 次仿真评估预算下显著超越标准贝叶斯优化（厚样品中位最终 SSE 最多降低 290 倍），且在实验数据上无需任务特定预训练即可获得一致参数估计，并能改善下游叠层衍射重建的图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电子显微术等材料表征中，从科学图像估计物理参数（如样品厚度、晶体失配角）通常依赖昂贵且耗时的物理仿真。传统网格搜索扩展性差，而神经网络方法需要大量预训练且难以迁移到新条件。因此需要一种仿真高效、无需预训练且能处理高维结构化输出的逆向求解方法。

Method: SBOCF 利用图像匹配目标函数的已知复合结构：将 PACBED 图像表示为补丁级摘要（patch-level summaries）加上两个修正项，从而在保留原始像素级目标函数的同时，把建模输出维度从 24,649 大幅降至 11，并在贝叶斯优化框架下通过复合函数结构高效利用仿真中间信息。

Result: 在 50 次仿真评估预算下，SBOCF 在合成 SrTiO3 厚/薄样品基准上均优于采用期望提升的标准贝叶斯优化，厚样品情况下中位最终 SSE 最多降低 290 倍；在实验数据上无需任务特定预训练即可得到与先前报道一致的参数估计；在模拟失配样品中，使用 SBOCF 估计参数进行下游叠层重建可获得原本模糊但现被锐化还原的原子像。

Conclusion: SBOCF 通过压缩输出维度并利用目标函数的复合结构，大幅提升了昂贵仿真逆向问题的参数估计效率与精度，可在无预训练条件下推广到实验数据，并改善下游原子尺度重建质量，是处理昂贵仿真器与高维结构化输出逆向问题的有前景方案。

Abstract: Estimating physical parameters from scientific images is a common inverse problem in materials characterization that often relies on expensive physics-based simulations. In electron microscopy, specimen thickness and crystal mistilt are critical parameters that govern how electrons scatter through the sample, and therefore the accuracy of any atomic-scale structure recovered from it. They are commonly inferred by matching experimental position-averaged convergent-beam electron diffraction (PACBED) patterns to simulated ones, but grid searches scale poorly and neural-network methods require extensive pretraining that may not transfer to new conditions. Here, we propose scalable Bayesian optimization of composite functions (SBOCF), a simulation-efficient method that exploits the known composite structure of the image-matching objective and the intermediate information contained in simulated images. By representing PACBED images with patch-level summaries and two correction terms, SBOCF preserves the original pixel-wise objective while reducing the number of modeled outputs from 24,649 to 11. Under a budget of 50 simulator evaluations, SBOCF outperformed standard Bayesian optimization with expected improvement on synthetic SrTiO3 benchmarks with thick and thin specimens, reducing the median final SSE by up to 290x in the thick-sample case. On experimental data, SBOCF produced parameter estimates consistent with previously reported values without task-specific pretraining. For a simulated mistilted specimen, using the SBOCF estimates in a downstream ptychographic reconstruction recovered sharp atoms that were otherwise blurred. These results establish SBOCF as a promising approach for inverse problems involving expensive simulators and high-dimensional structured outputs.

</details>


### [223] [Online Non-Monotone DR-Submodular Maximization Matching the Offline $0.401$ Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02145)
*Vaneet Aggarwal,Yiyang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非负非单调DR-次模函数在d维单位立方体紧凸下闭子集上的在线最大化问题，将脱机可构造的0.401近似因子首次推广到在线对抗环境，提出基于加权在线学习器替代脱机盒步的新算法，在子线性近似遗憾下保持该因子，并给出调用次数与遗憾之间的批量权衡，以及随机分块下单点bandit结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知最佳的可构造脱机近似因子为0.401（在元可解性假设下），而相应的对抗性在线保证长期停留在1/e，存在明显的差距；且在线算法无法在变化的目标函数上运行脱机构造，需要新的机制在对抗变化下保持脱机系数并控制累积残差项。

Method: 提出一种在线算法：不直接对变化目标运行脱机构造，而是用加权在线学习器（weighted online learner）替代脱机且依赖目标的盒步，以累积方式控制所需的残差项；并给出精确的非对称平衡定理（asymmetric balance theorem），使脱机系数在对抗变化下得以保持。在此基础上通过批量（batching）实现调用-遗憾权衡，并在正锚点条件下采用随机分块（randomized blocking）处理单点bandit反馈。

Result: 在事后全信息值-预言机模型下，算法达到0.401因子且近似遗憾为子线性（条件无偏有界预言机反馈）。直接实现为O(T^{3/4})遗憾、每轮O(dT^{1/4})次预言机调用；对任意δ∈[0,1/4]，批量得到O(T^δ)次调用/轮与O(T^{4/5-δ/5})遗憾，含单次调用O(T^{4/5})端点；在正锚点条件下，随机分块以O(T^{5/6})单点bandit遗憾保持0.401因子。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在在线对抗环境中达到与脱机可构造结果一致的0.401近似因子，消除了在线与脱机保证之间的差距，并通过精确平衡定理与批量/分块技术实现了预言机调用量与遗憾之间的系统权衡，为DR-次模在线优化提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: We study online maximization of nonnegative, non-monotone DR-submodular functions over compact convex down-closed subsets of the $d$-dimensional unit cube. The best known constructive offline approximation factor is $0.401$ under the corresponding meta-solvability assumptions, whereas comparable adversarial online guarantees had remained at $1/e$. We show that this factor is also achievable online. In the post-decision full-information value-oracle model, our algorithm attains factor $0.401$ with sublinear approximate regret when oracle feedback is conditionally unbiased and bounded.
  The online algorithm does not run the offline construction on a changing objective. Instead, it replaces the offline objective-dependent box step by a weighted online learner that controls the required residual terms cumulatively. An exact asymmetric balance theorem preserves the offline coefficients despite adversarial variation. The direct implementation has $O(T^{3/4})$ regret and uses $O(dT^{1/4})$ oracle calls per round. More generally, for every $δ\in[0,1/4]$, batching gives $O(T^δ)$ calls per round and $O(T^{4/5-δ/5})$ regret, including a one-call $O(T^{4/5})$ endpoint. Under a positive-anchor condition, randomized blocking retains factor $0.401$ with $O(T^{5/6})$ one-point bandit regret.

</details>


### [224] [GeoSPRINT: Geometric Redundancy-Aware Step Pruning for Inference in Diffusion Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02160)
*Arpita Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoSPRINT（Geometric Step Pruning for Inference in Trajectories）是一个无需训练的非均匀采样调度框架，利用去噪轨迹的几何形状（通过潜在空间中的超平面性测试与QR分解实现）来判定冗余步骤，将更多采样步数分配给轨迹高曲率区域，从而在相同NFE预算下显著提升扩散模型的采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能生成高质量样本，但推理阶段由于需要大量顺序神经函数评估（NFE）而代价高昂。现有加速方法要么使用固定的跳步调度，要么基于局部数值误差自适应步长，要么需要额外训练，均存在局限，缺乏利用全局轨迹几何信息来指导采样步数分配的机制。

Method: GeoSPRINT是一种免训练框架：1）通过潜在空间中的超平面性测试（利用QR分解高效实现）检测几何上冗余的采样步；2）将得到的冗余度分布转化为非均匀采样调度，在轨迹高曲率区域分配更多步数；3）提出轨迹投影分数α_traj（残差方差度量），用于量化轨迹平直度并作为整流流质量的免模型诊断指标。

Result: 在CIFAR-10（32×32）、LSUN Church（256×256）和Stable Diffusion v1.5（512×512潜空间）上，GeoSPRINT在相同NFE预算下一致优于均匀DDIM调度。CIFAR-10上FID改善0.7-1.1（49-89 NFE），且NFE≥30时超越DPM-Solver++（尽管只用一阶DDIM求解器）；LSUN Church在52步时将FID从1.48降至1.26；Stable Diffusion v1.5上FID最多提升1.93。

Conclusion: 轨迹几何提供了分配推理步数的有效全局信号；在不重训练的情况下，合理设计的采样调度能显著提升扩散采样效率，验证了基于几何冗余度的全局调度策略的普适性与实用价值。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve high sample quality but remain expensive at inference time because sampling requires many sequential neural function evaluations (NFEs). Existing acceleration methods either use fixed step-skipping schedules, adapt step sizes based on local numerical error, or require additional training. We introduce GeoSPRINT (Geometric Step Pruning for Inference in Trajectories), a training-free framework for constructing non-uniform sampling schedules from the geometry of denoising trajectories. GeoSPRINT detects geometrically redundant steps using a hyperplanarity test in latent space, implemented efficiently via QR factorization, and converts the resulting redundancy profile into a sampling schedule that allocates more steps to high-curvature regions of the trajectory. In addition, we introduce the trajectory projection score $α_{\mathrm{traj}}$, a residual-variance metric that quantifies trajectory straightness and serves as a model-free diagnostic for rectified flow quality. Across CIFAR-10 ($32{\times}32$), LSUN Church ($256{\times}256$), and Stable Diffusion v1.5 ($512{\times}512$ latent), GeoSPRINT consistently improves over uniform DDIM (Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models) schedules at matched NFE budgets. On CIFAR-10, GeoSPRINT improves FID (Fréchet Inception Distance) by 0.7-1.1 over DDIM across 49-89 NFEs and surpasses DPM-Solver++ at NFE${\geq}30$ despite using a first-order DDIM solver. On LSUN Church, it reduces FID from 1.48 to 1.26 at 52 steps, and on Stable Diffusion v1.5 it achieves up to 1.93 FID improvement over DDIM. These results show that trajectory geometry provides a useful global signal for allocating inference steps and that schedule quality can substantially improve diffusion sampling efficiency without retraining.

</details>


### [225] [DMRL: Document-Mediated Reinforcement Learning for Skill Optimization in Advertising Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02170)
*Wei Zhang,Hongji Li,Song Sun,Peng Yu,Xue Yang,Lei Zhao,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出文档中介强化学习（DMRL），将技能文档优化建模为一系列结构化编辑动作，通过上层的策略优化（DRPO）与长期奖励预测器（LRP）解决信用分配与长期效果估计问题，并在大规模短视频广告平台完成部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告推荐需要持续调整复杂系统参数以平衡商业回报与用户体验。现有工作虽引入带技能文档的大语言模型来辅助这一劳动密集型过程，但技能优化仍主要依赖提示驱动，缺乏将奖励归因到具体文档编辑的原则性机制，导致优化过程难以精确控制。

Method: 提出DMRL技能自进化框架：将技能文档优化建模为结构化编辑动作序列。上层智能体执行受控文档编辑，冻结的下层任务智能体通过A/B测试评估编辑效果。包含两个关键组件：(1) 双相对策略优化（DRPO），一种后训练策略优化方法，用于稳健且风险感知的优势估计；(2) 长期奖励预测器（LRP），通过解耦表示学习与交叉注意力迁移建模群体异质性，以估计长期结果。

Result: DMRL部署于大规模短视频广告平台，大量实证评估表明其在关键广告指标上优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: DMRL为广告推荐中的技能文档优化提供了可归因、可评估的强化学习框架，通过DRPO与LRP克服了信用分配和长期回报估计两大难题，并在工业场景中验证了其有效性，为LLM驱动的技能自进化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Advertising recommendation requires continuously tuning complex system parameters while balancing commercial returns and user experience. Recent work has introduced large language models (LLMs) with skill documents to assist this labor-intensive process, but skill optimization remains largely prompt-driven, lacking a principled mechanism to attribute rewards to specific document edits. To address this limitation, we propose Document-Mediated Reinforcement Learning (DMRL), a skill self-evolution framework that models skill document optimization as a sequence of structured editing actions. In DMRL, an upper-level agent performs controlled document edits, while a frozen lower-level task agent evaluates their effects through A/B testing. To address credit assignment and long-term outcomes, we introduce two key components: (1) Dual-Relative Policy Optimization (DRPO), a post-training policy optimization method for robust and risk-aware advantage estimation; and (2) Long-term Reward Predictor (LRP), which estimates long-term outcomes by modeling population heterogeneity with disentangled representation learning and cross-attention transfer. DMRL was deployed on a large-scale short-video ads platform and extensive empirical evaluation shows that DMRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across key advertising metrics

</details>


### [226] [SMart: A Multi-source Multi-phase Time Series Representation Transfer Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02203)
*Fang He,Wang-chien Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMart框架，一种多源多阶段时间序列表示迁移方法，通过多阶段递归图恢复任务和多源数据集选择器，提升时间序列表示学习的质量，在分类与回归任务上超越多个现有最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列表示学习（TSRL）近年受到广泛关注，现有两条探索路径：一是基于Transformer框架学习时间序列；二是借用其他数据集进行表示迁移。然而，前者采用的自监督时间序列恢复任务在技术上较为简单，后者仅使用单一源数据集，两者均有提升空间。因此需要更复杂的自监督任务和利用多源数据的新机制来增强TSRL。

Method: 提出新的TSRL框架SMart（multi-source multi-phase time series representation transfer），包含两个新颖机制：1）多阶段递归图（recurrence plots）恢复任务，提供三种替代模式，引导编码器将时间序列动态嵌入到表示中；2）源数据集选择器，从多个候选数据集中筛选合适的源数据集，用于预训练TSRL编码器，以补充原始目标数据集。

Result: 实验结果表明，SMart在单变量和多变量时间序列数据集上的表示学习、分类和回归任务中均优于多个现有最优模型；对于时间序列回归任务，平均绝对误差降低最高达19.5%；对于时间序列分类任务，平均准确率提升最高达1.34%。

Conclusion: SMart通过更精细的多阶段递归图恢复自监督任务与多源数据集选择机制，有效弥补了现有TSRL方法在任务复杂度和数据来源单一性上的不足，验证了利用多源数据和多阶段动力学建模能够显著提升时间序列表示学习的性能。

Abstract: Time series representation learning (TSRL) has attracted growing research interests in recent years. Two recent explorations in TSRL are: i) exploiting a transformer-based framework to learn time series; ii) instead of using only the targeted dataset, borrowing time series from other datasets to to facilitate representation transfer. While these two explorations are shown effective, the self-supervised time series recovery task in (i) and the single-source dataset used in (ii) are technically simple and thus can be enhanced with new ideas. In this work, we propose a new TSRL framework, namely multi-source multi-phase time series representation transfer (SMart), which has two novel mechanisms to address the aforementioned deficiencies: 1) a multi-phase recurrence plots recovery task, in three alternative modes, for guiding the encoder to embed time series dynamics into the time series representation; and 2) a source dataset selector to select multiple suitable source datasets to supplement the original target dataset for pre-training the TSRL encoder. Experimental results show that SMart outperforms several state-of-the-art models for time series representation learning, classification and regression on both uni-variate and multi-variate time series datasets, reducing mean absolute error up to 19.5% for time series regression, and increasing average accuracy up to 1.34\% for time series classification.

</details>


### [227] [Recursive Value Learning for Long-Horizon Offline Goal-Conditioned RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02237)
*Hyeonseong Jeon,Youngwoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DCRL（分治强化学习），通过将轨迹段递归分解为平衡二叉树、按叶到根顺序训练价值函数，将离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）扩展到长程任务，显著降低自举深度与误差累积，在OGBench长程任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线长程GCRL难以规模化，原因有二：一是长程价值学习依赖的短程估计仍可能不准确，误差沿长链传播；二是基于max的价值备份会通过重复传播放大过估计问题。这些因素导致现有扁平式离线GCRL方法在长程目标上表现不佳。

Method: DCRL采用分而治之策略：递归地将每条轨迹片段分解为平衡二叉树，并按照从叶节点到根节点的顺序训练价值函数。每个父节点只在子节点更新完成后被更新，且利用观测路线（demonstrated route）的精确因子分解而非在含噪候选中进行选择。由于该目标沿演示路线学习而非强制最优，DCRL还在不同轨迹间联合传播价值，以发现更短路径。平衡二叉树结构将最坏情况下的自举深度由线性降为对数。

Result: 减少了自举依赖深度（线性降至对数级），对应更慢的经验误差累积；在多种目标到达任务中显著优于现有扁平式离线GCRL方法；在OGBench五个最具挑战性的长程任务上，将最优基线平均得分从55提升至64，超越所有扁平与层次化基线。

Conclusion: 分治式的树状价值学习结构能有效缓解长程离线GCRL中的误差传播与过估计放大问题，验证了缩短自举依赖深度对提升长程任务性能的关键作用，为离线长程决策提供了有效思路。

Abstract: Scaling offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) to long-horizon tasks is difficult because (1) long-range value learning depends on shorter-range estimates that may still be inaccurate, and (2) max-based value backups can amplify overestimation through repeated propagation. We propose DCRL (Divide-and-Conquer RL), which recursively decomposes each trajectory segment into a balanced binary tree and trains the values from leaves to root. Each parent is therefore updated only after its children, using an exact factorization of the observed route rather than selecting among noisy alternatives. Since this objective learns values along demonstrated routes that are not necessarily optimal, DCRL jointly propagates values across trajectories to discover shorter routes. Thanks to the balanced binary tree, DCRL reduces worst-case bootstrap depth from linear to logarithmic, and this shorter dependency structure empirically corresponds to much slower error accumulation.
